The admin page shows a "Debug" badge in the list and on the player page; unmarking sits behind typing the player's name. |
| `DELETE /players/{id}` | Closes its WebSocket, deletes the account (cascade: sessions, characters). |
| `DELETE /characters/{id}` | Any account's character. Whoever plays it is logged out first, then it is deleted and forgotten by the lobby. |
| `GET /actions` | The last 100 audit rows. |

Every change writes a row to `admin_actions` (admin, action, target name at that time, detail) and counts `ta.admin.actions`.

**Tracking (migrations `0002_admin_stats.sql`, `0003_run_players_left.sql`, `0004_runs_level.sql`).**
- `activity_days(account_id, day)`: one row per account and UTC day, written at login, registration and WebSocket connect (`db::touch_activity`, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`). "Active players" means accounts that logged in or connected.
- `runs` + `run_players`: every ended run (`victory`, `defeat` or `abandoned` = everyone left) with level, boss (`NULL` for a level without one; 0004 made it nullable and added `level`, default `'Dungeon'` for older rows), length, party size and per player the class, kills, damage, healing, XP, coins, `survived` and `left_run`.
  - Leaving a dungeon counts as dying inside the game (`Player.alive = false`). `Player.left` keeps it apart, so the statistics count deaths as `NOT survived AND NOT left_run`.
  - Debug runs are stored with `debug = true` and left out of every run statistic. The statistics that sum over characters (total XP and coins, characters per class, tree-node levels, the top 10) leave out characters of debug accounts; account counts and active players still count debug accounts (they count logins).
- These tables have **no foreign keys** to accounts or characters. When an account is deleted its name goes with it and only an anonymous id stays, so past counts do not change.
- History starts empty when this is first deployed; the dashboard says so.

**Statistics (`stats::overview`).**
- Calendar days in UTC: "today" starts at 00:00 UTC, "7 days" is today and the six days before.
- **Live:** from `Lobby::live()` (online, in a dungeon, in the lobby, rooms waiting, dungeons running, and who is online with which character), plus account names from the database.
- **Totals:** accounts, characters, XP ever earned, unspent coins, runs.
- **Today / 7 / 30 days:** active players, new accounts, runs (won, lost, left), win rate over finished runs, average length and party size, kills, deaths.
- **Per level and per class** (30 days; "Levels" lists each level with its boss, e.g. "Dungeon – Demon", from `AdminStats.level_runs`; `AdminStats.levels` is the tree-nodes histogram), characters by number of tree nodes bought, the top 10 characters by XP earned, and 30 daily points of active players, new accounts and runs (won vs. the rest).
- Charts are hand-written SVG (`charts.ts`), drawn at the container's pixel width. Each has a hover and keyboard tooltip and a "Show as table" view.

## 11c. OpenTelemetry

`telemetry::init()` (first thing in `main`) always sets up the console log (`RUST_LOG`, default `info`). It also exports over **OTLP/HTTP (protobuf)**, but only when `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` (or a per-signal endpoint) is set.

- **Endpoint:** the exporter appends `/v1/traces`, `/v1/logs` and `/v1/metrics` and honours the standard `OTEL_*` variables (`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS`, `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME`, `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES`, …). `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME` defaults to `tiny-adventurers-server`.
- **No TLS:** no TLS client is compiled in, so the endpoint must be plain `http://`, typically a collector on the same Docker network. For HTTPS, enable the `reqwest-rustls` feature of `opentelemetry-otlp`.
- **ClickStack:** point the endpoint at its OTel collector, e.g. `http://otel-collector:4318`. Add the game to that network or publish the collector's 4318 port, and pass `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS=authorization=<ingestion key>` if the collector requires one.
- **Shutdown:** on Ctrl-C or SIGTERM the server stops and flushes what is still buffered (`Guard::shutdown`).

**Traces** (`tracing` spans bridged by `tracing-opentelemetry`):
- `http`: one span per request, named by the matched route and recording the status. Cookies and bodies are not recorded.
- `ws`: one span per WebSocket session, with the account id.
- `run`: one span per run, with the run id, level, boss, player count and outcome.
- One span per `db.rs` function (`#[tracing::instrument(skip_all)]`) and per admin query in `stats.rs`.

**Logs:** every `tracing` event at `info` and above becomes an OTLP log record, correlated with its span. The exporter's own HTTP client crates are filtered out (`OTEL_FILTER`) to avoid a feedback loop.

**Metrics** (every 15 s; attributes carry ids, classes, levels, bosses and outcomes, never player names):

| Instrument | Type | Attributes |
|---|---|---|
| `ta.players.online` | gauge, read from the lobby | `state` = lobby / in_run |
| `ta.runs.active` | gauge | `state` = running / waiting |
| `ta.logins` | counter | `result` = ok / fail / throttled |
| `ta.registrations` | counter | – |
| `ta.runs.finished` | counter | `level`, `boss` (only when the level has one), `outcome`, `debug` |
| `ta.run.duration` (s), `ta.run.players` | histograms | `level`, `boss`, `outcome` / – |
| `ta.player.deaths`, `ta.enemies.killed`, `ta.xp.banked`, `ta.coins.banked` | counters | `class` |
| `ta.tick.duration` (ms) | histogram, every server tick | – |
| `ta.ws.connections` | up-down counter | – |
| `ta.admin.actions` | counter | `action` |

Debug runs only count in `ta.runs.finished{debug=true}`.

**Trying it locally:**
1. Run a throwaway collector that prints what it receives (`otel/opentelemetry-collector` with an `otlp` HTTP receiver on 4318 and the `debug` exporter) on the compose network (`--network tinyadventurers_default --name otel-check`).
2. Start the game with `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT=http://otel-check:4318`.
3. Play, then read `docker logs otel-check`.

## 11d. Rules log

A toggleable text log that tells the player why a hit was 9 and not 12 and why an effect did or did not take. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-rules-log-design.md`. It is built to be removable: it lives in two folders and the game reaches it through a fixed list of one-line hooks, pinned by a boundary test on each side.

### The two folders

- `server/src/run/rules_log/`: `mod.rs` (the hook functions, `RulesLog` state on `Run`, the facts structs), `record.rs` (`LogRec`, `LineKind`, `Seen`/`Unseen`: what is kept until the snapshot), `text.rs` (all wording and number formatting), `tags.rs` (creature numbering), `send.rs` (the per-viewer filter, `LogW`/`LineW`, the byte cap), `tests.rs` (including the boundary test).
- `client/src/rulesLog/`: `index.ts` (`RulesLog`: keys, opt-in message, ties it together), `buffer.ts` (the last 300 records; `push` returns what to append and how many to trim), `prefs.ts` (localStorage `ta.rulesLog`), `panel.ts` + `rulesLog.css` (the DOM panel, bottom-left, Inter 11 px with tabular digits; inserted before `#ui`, so the pause menu and end screen paint over it), `tags.ts` (the tag texts above figures), tests including `boundary.test.ts`.

### How it works

- **Opt-in.** The client sends `ClientMsg::RulesLog { on, details }` (on `L` / `Shift+L`, and once at the start of a run if the saved prefs say on). `main.rs` forwards it to the run, which stores it per player (`set_prefs`). `rules_log::on(run)` is false when nobody has the log on: every hook returns at once, nothing is recorded and `Snapshot.log` is `None`.
- **Recording.** The hooks sit inside the game's own computations (`hurt_player`, `hurt_monster`, `Run::apply`, `heal_*`, the ability executors) and are handed facts the game already computed; they never mutate the game. The six fingerprint tests pass unchanged with the log on and off.
- **Visibility rule.** A record is sent to a player only if that player sees every creature it names at that moment (party members always count as seen; a gone creature never does, so the record is unseeable). Otherwise it is dropped, except damage the viewer (or their wolf) took: that gives the record's short `unseen` line, with the viewer's own resistance steps and no attacker data. An Immune or Healed hit from something unseen gives no line. An effect that rides on a hit from something unseen adds only its name to the short line ("… from something unseen · Chilled"), never a detail line (Ruling 11): the details carry the attacker's power. A pure effect hit (no damage: the lich's Sleep orb) carries its attacker as the record's origin (`hit_effects`), so it is judged like any record and a hero is not told about an unseen attacker's effect. A hidden mimic is tagged and named as a chest until it is revealed. A spectator gets what the spectated hero gets.
- **The cap.** `LOG_BYTES_PER_SNAP` = 4096 text bytes per snapshot; the rest is summed up in a closing note ("… n more records (log limit)"; it counts records, not their detail lines). The client keeps the last 300 records and its panel is append-only: a snapshot adds its rows at the bottom and trims the top beyond the cap; only a details toggle or a clear rebuilds it. With details off the server does not send the detail lines at all. **Bandwidth bound:** at most about 4 KB of text per snapshot and listener, so about 4 KB × snapshots per second (30) per player with the log on, in the worst case; with the log off nothing is sent.
- **Text.** All wording is in `text.rs`; the client formats nothing. A line is `LineW(time, kind, headline, details)`. Numbers are rounded to one decimal; multipliers show two (×0.49). Effect lines are details of their hit, with a headline suffix (" · Chilled"); a hit with several effects gets one line per applied effect (#197). A damage-over-time tick names its bestower (`Why.by` is the effect's source), so it reads "Goblin 1 ▸ Poisoned ▸ Ayla" and also shows in the bestower's log (`hit_begin` adds `seen_by_id(by)` to `who`). A viewer who cannot see the bestower (out of sight, invisible, gone) gets the anonymous "Poisoned ▸ Ayla" instead: the record's `plain` names the bestower as optional (`send.rs` skips it in the visibility check and swaps the head), and a death by a tick never names it, so it does not wait on it. A tick with no recorded bestower stays anonymous.
- **Tags.** `tags.rs` numbers each creature once at spawn, per name ("Goblin 3"), as a plain counter whether or not the log is on, so numbers stay stable when someone turns it on mid-run. The snapshot carries `[entity, kind, number]` for the sent entities; `TAG_NAME` in the generated `defs.ts` gives the names. The client draws the tags above the figures only while the log is on, at the text resolution zoom x camera zoom x devicePixelRatio so they are sharp at the screen's real pixels (the world texts use the fixed `TEXT_RESOLUTION` instead, see §9).
- **Wire.** `Snapshot.log: Option<LogW>` is the eleventh element of `SnapW`. A hand-written `Serialize` for `SnapW` in `wire.rs` writes 10 elements while the log is off and 11 while it is on (serde cannot skip a trailing tuple field). This code is log-only but outside the module and not counted by the boundary test. The generated `SnapW.ts` types the 11th element as `LogW | null` although it is absent while off; `client/src/wire.ts` uses `w[10] ?? null`.

### Server hooks (as built)

Every line outside `run/rules_log/` that contains `rules_log` or `RulesLog` (`grep -rn "rules_log\|RulesLog" server/src --include=*.rs | grep -v "run/rules_log/"`); the boundary test `rules_log_is_reached_only_through_its_hooks` pins the count per file.

| File | Lines | What |
|---|---|---|
| `protocol.rs` | 2 | `ClientMsg::RulesLog { on, details }`; `Snapshot.log: Option<LogW>` |
| `wire.rs` | 1 | the `Option<LogW>` field of `SnapW` (plus the hand-written `Serialize`, not counted) |
| `main.rs` | 1 | the arm forwarding `ClientMsg::RulesLog` to the run (Ruling 5) |
| `export.rs` | 2 | `export_tag_names()` into `defs.ts`; a `LogW` in the wire fixtures |
| `run/mod.rs` | 20 | `pub mod rules_log`; the `log: RulesLog` field and its `default()`; `spawned` (monster spawn); `set_prefs` in the message handler; `forget` when a player leaves; `revealed` for a mimic; in `hurt_player` and `hurt_monster`: five `hit_begin` calls (player healed; player dealt, immune or debug; boss shielded, not yet hurtable; monster healed; monster dealt or immune), five `hit_end` (three share a line with their `hit_begin`), two `death`; `heal` in `heal_player` and `heal_monster` |
| `run/visibility.rs` | 2 | `take` the records once per snapshot send (every 2 ticks); `for_viewer` per player snapshot |
| `run/levels/boss.rs` | 1 | `spawned` for the boss |
| `run/objects.rs` | 1 | `spawned` for barrels and chests |
| `defs/effects.rs` | 1 | `effect_name` for the log's wording of an effect |
| `run/effects.rs` | 10 | `effect` after `apply` decided the outcome; `reveal`, `end_effects` and `remove_effect_why` take an `EndReason` (and the last calls `ended`); `attrs_changed`; the Water and RanOut `ended` calls |
| `run/magic.rs` | 2 | test lines (`take`) |
| `run/companion.rs` | 3 | `tags::tag_name` for the recruit and fallen messages; a test line |
| `run/charm.rs` | 6 | the `use`; `charm` when a charm comes on and when it ends; test lines |
| `run/abilities.rs` | 23 | `too_close` for the melee-blocked secondary; `cast` after the cooldown is set (the fallback Strike and the normal use, heroes only; it logs the cooldown and its `CdSteps`, #198); the `rules_log::on(run)` guards and `skipped` calls (area abilities, Chain Spark); `EndReason::Purify` in Purify; and the log's own test lines at the end of the file |

(75 lines in all. The counts are the ones the test pins; the test and `grep` are the authority if they differ.)

### Client hooks

`grep -rn "rulesLog\|RulesLog\|w\[10\]" client/src --include=*.ts | grep -v "src/rulesLog/" | grep -v generated` finds `wire.ts:138` (`log: w[10] ?? null`) and seven lines in two files (#302). `game/PhaserRunView.ts` has five: the import, the field, creation in the constructor, `draw` each frame with the entity views (mobs, mimics, chests and barrels, so a real chest and a hidden mimic get the same tag at the same height, `CHEST_HEIGHT`), `destroy` in `PhaserRunView.destroy`. `game/GameScene.ts` has two, in `create`: `view.rulesLog.onSnapshot(m)` on each `Snap` and `hud.setToggles(view.rulesLog.settings())`. The session and `run/` do not name the log. The generated `defs.ts` also carries `TAG_NAME`.

### Game data the log reads

The log reads these; they stay useful without it. `Damage.why` (`Why`, `What`, `Mult`: who, with what, which multipliers); `Base` (given, ratio, ability mult, mods mult, `keystone_mult` for the static keystones; `Base::amount()` is the damage) on `ResolvedWeapon` and `ResolvedAbility`, built in `resolve_parts`; `Attr::short`; `EffectOutcome`, `Duration`, `apply_traced`, `Apply.origin` and `effect_left` in effects; `run/targets.rs` (why a target was skipped); the `by` parameter of `heal_player`/`heal_monster` and the private `mend_player`; `#[cfg(test)] Run.last_hit`.

### How to add a line or a reason

- A new reason for an effect that was refused, replaced or ended: add a variant to the outcome enum (`EffectOutcome`) or to `EndReason`; the compiler lists every `match` to extend (no `_ =>` arms), then put the wording in `text.rs`.
- A new damage step or multiplier: add it to `Mult` (or `Base`; the run-time keystones are `Mult::Keystone(Keystone, factor)`, printed as "<name> ×1.25") where the game computes it, and write its detail line in `text.rs`. The logged final number must stay the damage actually dealt; extend the `dealt` tests.
- A new kind of event: a facts struct and a hook function in `rules_log/mod.rs`, a `LineKind`, and one hook line at the site. Add the line to the boundary test's per-file count and to the table above. Every record names the creatures in it (`Seen`), so the visibility rule applies without further work; add the line kind to the leak test.
- A new creature kind needs a name in `tags.rs::tag_name` (a compile error tells you).

### How to remove the rules log

1. Delete both folders: `server/src/run/rules_log/` and `client/src/rulesLog/`.
2. Delete every hook line in the two tables: `grep -rn "rules_log\|RulesLog" server/src --include=*.rs` and `grep -rn "rulesLog\|RulesLog\|w\[10\]" client/src --include=*.ts` (the second also finds `wire.ts` `log: w[10] ?? null`). Where a hook sits inside the game's code, take only the hook out:
   - `effects.rs`: `remove_effect_why` loses its `EndReason` parameter (keep the removal itself); delete the Water and RanOut `ended` calls and the `effect` call after `apply`; the Water and Purify callers of `remove_effect_why` drop their `EndReason` argument (`abilities.rs` ~391 too), and in `abilities.rs` the `too_close` call (~129) goes.
   - `abilities.rs`: in the four area executors (Frost Nova and War Cry in `use_ring`, `land_slam` for Ground Slam, and `melee_arc`), delete the `let log = ...on(run);` line and the `skipped` call, and pass `false` to `targets::select` (or drop its `collect` parameter and edit `targets.rs`). In `chain_jumps` also delete `skips`/`stop` (`let (mut skips, mut stop)`, the `targets::merge` line, the `if log { stop = targets::nearest_beyond(..) }` block inside the `let Some(mi) = next else`, the `skips.retain` line and the `skipped` call); the `else { break; }` stays.
   - `run/mod.rs`: delete the `hit_begin`, `hit_end`, `death`, `heal`, `spawned`, `revealed`, `forget` and `set_prefs` lines, the `log` field and its `default()`, and `pub mod rules_log`.
3. Drop what names the log without the grep strings:
   - `protocol.rs`: `ClientMsg::RulesLog` and `Snapshot.log`.
   - `wire.rs`: the eleventh `SnapW` field and the hand-written `Serialize` for `SnapW` (back to a derive); `s.log.clone()` in the `SnapW(...)` call (~143); `log: None,` in `sample_snapshots` (~206 and ~238).
   - `main.rs`: `ClientMsg::RulesLog { .. }` in the forwarding arm.
   - `export.rs`: the `export_tag_names()` line (~213, and the `TAG_NAME` export); in `export_wire_fixtures` the `let mut with_log = ...` (~439), the `with_log.log = ...` line (~440) and `samples.push(with_log)` (~442; then `samples` can be the plain `sample_snapshots()`).
   - `run/mod.rs` tests: `level_setup_is_unchanged_with_the_log_on`, `simulation_is_unchanged_after_waking_the_boss_with_the_log_on`, `plan_3a_fight_is_pinned_with_the_log_on`, the `run.log.forced` lines (~1499, ~1545, ~1568) and the `fight_with_log` helper (~1566; make `fight` build the run directly).
   - Client: `src/wire.test.ts` (~96-110): the two log tests, and the `cases.length === 3` expectation (back to 2); the `log` field of `Snapshot` in `wire.ts`.
   Regenerate the bindings with the docker suite and commit `client/src/generated`.
4. Then run `cargo build`, `cargo test -- --skip export_bindings`, the docker suite, `tsc --noEmit` and `vitest`, and fix what the compiler names; the sites above are the known ones.
5. Optional cleanup (harmless if kept): `Damage.why`, `Base` and `resolve_parts`, `Attr::short`, `EffectOutcome`/`Duration`/`apply_traced`/`Apply.origin`/`effect_left`, `run/targets.rs`, the `by` parameter of the heals and `mend_player`, `Run.last_hit`. Then remove the "Rules log" rows and section of the player guide and this section.

## 12. Tests

**Server (`cargo test`, 1198 tests with the export tests; 1131 with `-- --skip export_bindings`, which is what a sandbox without docker runs):**
- sides and companions (#149; `run/targets.rs`, `run/companion.rs`, `run/wolf.rs`): `foes` and `side` for every pair; a non-wild Neutral creature takes no damage and no effect and does nothing; recruiting with E (after waking teammates); the leader hierarchy (recruiter, host, next hero; a summoned one vanishes with its summoner); the knight's spot; a recruited knight fights, follows and costs nothing when it falls; `EntSnap.side`; the wolf's tests below:
- creature layers (#227; `run/behaviour.rs`, `run/modules.rs`, `run/ai.rs`): the role of each creature kind, kit order for every enemy type, `chase_of` per creature, the Leap (start, hold after a hit, overrun, the rescue from a chasm, water and lava, and not while asleep, petrified or frightened), escorts counting their waits down.
- wildlife (#227; `run/behaviour.rs`, `run/targets.rs`, `run/visibility.rs`, `run/mod.rs`, `dungeon/cove.rs`): a calm animal wanders and is ignored; a bunny or deer flees for the minimum time and again on a new hit; a boar hunts the side that hit it, or the enemy that did, goes home and wanders; the death rules; grudges and `fights`; chains skip a calm animal; a calm animal is sent Neutral with the wild flag and a grudging boar Hostile; the cove spawns its 15 animals after the knight.
- the wolf (`run/wolf.rs`): summon, heal and the 60 s wait; follow, guard, dash, bite, leash, reappear and the chasm escape; no hero attack hurts the wolf; enemies and area attacks do; vanishing with the ranger
- incorporeal (#168): the Undead + Incorporeal worked example and the cap (`races.rs`, `damage.rs`); resistance stacking unchanged (Lich, Silk Robe + Warded); phasing through a wall but never stopping in one, hovering over terrain, no knockback, Entangled immunity; Invisible's reveal rules, `go_invisible` and its cooldown, `Skip::Invisible`, snapshot filtering; `debug_incorporeal_only_in_debug_runs`; client `entityAlpha.test.ts`
- ambush (#169, #170; `run/ambush.rs`): the straggler (furthest from the centre, alone, tie to the nearest); vanish on sight but not when only a companion is in sight, never mid wind-up; drift through a wall to the straggler; the 0.5 s warning before the strike and dodging it; giving up after the Invisible time; re-vanishing when ready; Ghost Touch drains STR and Shade Touch CON; `defs/kinds.rs` `spectres_have_a_body_height`
- haunts (#169, #170): `place_haunts` and `haunts_leave_the_map_unchanged` (`dungeon/generate.rs`), `only_the_dragon_has_no_haunt` (`defs/enemies.rs`), `lich_dungeons_get_ghosts_and_dragon_dungeons_none` and `demon_dungeons_get_shades` (`run/levels/mod.rs`)
- damage model: `resolve` for single and several types, a missing entry, immunity, the 100 % cap (`resistance_is_capped_at_100_percent`, `full_resistance_is_immunity_and_more_is_capped`: no resistance heals), a weakness and its −100 % floor, partial splits filled with physical; attacks by kind have their types; race resistances add up with adjustments (lich physical 50 %, dragon heat 100 %), every enemy and class has its entity type; typed paths (a demon takes no heat from the fireball, a skeleton takes more, the demon's claw burns, lava, the lich nova and the dragon's breath are typed)
- world objects: chests open once and pay the party, mimics still wake, objects resist poison fully
- #191 (bodiless): `is_attack_by_def`, `haste_keeps_invisible`, `war_cry_frightens_and_reveals_invisible`, `passes_through_reads_the_final_resistance`, `shot_flies_through_bodiless_to_next`, `phaser_passes_every_blocking_object`, `can_stop_follows_dash_blocking`, `barrel_blast_and_hazard_pass_a_bodiless_monster` (HP, Invisible and knock unchanged); client `splashes` (`entityAlpha.ts`)
- chests: any hit (not poison) smashes a closed chest for a third of its coins, once, with the splinter `Boom`; an opened chest ignores hits; a broken chest pays nothing on touch; a shot stops at a closed chest and flies over an opened one; a sword breaks one; the dagger stabs a closed chest in reach even when aimed away
- solids: a square blocks axis by axis, a figure inside one can walk out in every direction; 36 fixture steps touch a solid
- barrels: lit by any hit but poison, explode after 0.3 s, one explosion per barrel in a chain (ten in a row give ten `Boom k 7`); the blast hurts and pushes a hero and a non-boss monster, hurts a boss without pushing it, does not reach a monster behind a wall, and gives the kill and damage to the igniter; a chain without an igniter or whose igniter left still explodes; the E push, its reach, stops at a wall and at another barrel (which stays put); a pushed barrel rests on the 1/16 px wire grid (`q_pos` round-trips it exactly); falls into a chasm, sinks in water, explodes in lava; every hero attack kind hits objects, shots stop at barrels; area attacks light barrels: ogre slam (in reach, not outside), dragon breath (in the cone, not outside), dragon smash and claw, demon cleave (in front, not behind), lich nova (in range, not outside), fire patches; `objects_in` reach and cone
- monsters: blown into a chasm (one death, XP once), into water, into lava (burns, walks out); bosses are never pushed
- ranged AI: shoots a barrel next to the hero, does not shoot one that would catch it, ignores a far one; A* goes around barrels; a wide boss reaches every spot of its hall with barrels in it
- barrel generation over many seeds: no tile cut off, hall barrels keep their plain-floor area and gap
- light kinds: every kind has a sane def; fire and magic shots glow, arrows and monsters do not
- light registry: a static light lights its radius and stops at walls (lighting the wall itself); switching, recolouring (`Ev::Light` with the colour) and removing a static light; lights added at the start send no event; a moving light follows its entity, is blocked by walls and is swept when its entity is gone; it lights exactly its radius-r shadowcast from its tile (not a tile diagonally past it, but every point of a lit tile); it is cast from the tile of its sent position (47.99 px goes out as 48); kind colour unless overridden; fire bolts carry a light, arrows do not, a shot that dies at once leaves none; fire patches glow; lava lights its neighbours, is in the start info (also in a dungeon) and `set_tile` keeps the lava lights in step; a closed door blocks light; an explosive shot hitting a wall in the dark lights a spot three tiles from it for the flash's 0.4 s, not after, and the flash is swept; a paladin's healing wave lights a spot three tiles away for 0.6 s; a fireball aimed a hair inside a wall goes off in front of it (its flash is not inside the wall)
- vision and darkvision: `sees` (within vision, line of sight, lit or within vision × darkvision; humans never see an unlit hero, monsters only within a third, undead and demons fully), enemy AI in the dark by type, the hero snapshot filter with `dark_reach` (also a spectator with the teammate's sight), `SelfW` darkvision, `DebugDarkvision` (honoured only when debug is allowed, marks the run as debug, spectators get the teammate's value), the shared `darkvision` fixture
- visibility: a monster in the dark is hidden until lit; end to end through `send_snapshots`, a dark viewer gets a monster lit only by a fire patch but not one just outside the patch's shadowcast; recolouring a moving light changes its snapshot entry and sends no event; hero events (hurt, heal) are sent in the dark, monster events are not; the moving-light list holds lights near the viewer, with the colour only when custom; a torch in sight is sent lit or dark and one out of sight is not
- torches: a torch lights the floor in front of it; E toggles one in reach; heat lights and other damage puts out; a barrel blast lights a torch; a shot into the torch's wall tile hits it; a barrel blast adds a `Blast` flash on its spot (a moving light)
- torch generation over 200 seeds: on walls with floor in front, spaced, never adjacent, never on doorway jambs, start room and boss hall always lit, dark pockets exist; spacing along the start room wall; the fingerprints are unchanged
- effects (`defs/effects.rs`, `run/effects.rs`): the damage-type rows and `type_effects` (half a hit is enough; `TYPE_EFFECTS` matches the defs), damage over time (ticks, effect damage applies no effects, resistance scales it), refusal at full resistance to the type, the cancels, water puts out fire, a burn kill credits `last_hit_by`, `resolve_stat` (add, then multiply, then the lowest set; the resistance cap keeps immunity), the flags of each kind, effect resistances (default 0, half the time at 0.5, refused at 1, a negative value at most doubles, applied after the save, the boss table, none set for a damage-type effect), apply, refresh to the longer time, remove and the events, the dead and sinking lose theirs, the tick, hit effects with and without a roll, a hero asleep or stone cannot move, act or (stone) turn, speed and knockback factors, blind and asleep vision, a teammate in reach wakes a sleeper before any object (out of reach does nothing; a sleeper cannot wake another), a hit that kills applies no effect, the debug message only when allowed; damage-type effects at the source's governing Attribute (`run/abilities.rs`: the Fireball at INT 15 and 18, fire bolts at the imp's CHA, the summoner's INT and at least 10, an unknown owner, lava and a barrel at 10; `run/bosses/dragon.rs`: the breath at CHA 20)
- lich spells: each spell's orb or gaze puts its effect on the hero, a gaze broken by line of sight does nothing, a hero who already suffers an effect is not picked again, the bosses' effect resistances
- protocol round trip
- wire: positions round to 1/16 px (also beyond 4096 px), aim wraps into a byte, event codes follow `EV_CODES` (the light events and `Snapshot.lights` are in the sample snapshots), a compact input decodes to `InputMsg`, sample snapshots are bare arrays and at least 30 % smaller than the old form
- dungeon determinism and guarantees over 200 seeds
- level split: the fingerprints `level_setup_is_unchanged` (map, spawns, entity ids and boss per seed) and `simulation_is_unchanged_after_waking_the_boss` (300 steps of a boss fight), recorded before the split and re-recorded for the knight in #149 (a2a92d9, with their log-on twins; `plan_3a_fight_is_pinned` did not change) (`level_setup_is_unchanged` leaves barrels and their ids out and so keeps its values from before barrels; the simulation one was re-recorded with barrels, which change the fight); `Dungeon::split` keeps every part; `Level::build` matches the generator and draws a random boss from the run's rng as before; a level with only default hooks has no boss bar or debug path and is lost when everyone dies; start and end messages name the level and boss
- collision cannot tunnel through walls; line of sight
- knockback: a push slides its distance, ignores the input, crosses deep water and stops at walls; a push into a chasm is a fall, with no attacks meanwhile, and dashing heroes are not pushed; a short push stops and control returns; only orc warriors' and chorts' melee pushes (not imps, skeletons, archers or bolts)
- ogre: slams a crowd (or one hero while the club recovers) and clubs a lone hero; the slam hits and pushes every hero in its radius straight away and spares those outside; club and slam both push; exactly one ogre per seed on safe ground with room around it, outside the boss hall; the debug path also leads to the ogre and disappears when it dies
- wide bosses (demon, dragon) reach every spot of their hall around the chasms (24 seeds)
- terrain: the rule matrix per mover; slowing in shallow water; a dash over a chasm and deep water; a chasm kills after the fall (not mid-dash) and debug heroes climb back out; a dash ending in deep water drowns; lava burns over time; only demons are lava walkers; A* keeps enemies out of lava and chasms and lets demons through lava
- terrain keeps the layout and chests of a seed for both themes
- FOV blocked by walls
- ranger dagger vs crossbow (no dagger target on allies)
- cooldown enforcement
- enemy ignores players without line of sight; never attacks unseen players
- necromancer death kills its minions
- minions (`run/minions.rs`, `run/ai.rs`): `room` counts only the caster's living minions of the raised types, `raise` balances the types and ties go to the melee skeleton, a blocked ring spot falls back to the caster's spot, the AI casts with a wind-up and the cast lands at the last known position, no room spends no cooldown, the numbers per creature, `per_cast`/`alive` follow INT and `PerCastAdd`/`AliveAdd` (clamped at 0)
- demon dungeon swap (same spawns, imps/chorts/summoners instead of skeletons/necromancers, none elsewhere); imp and chort attack choice by distance; the alt attack's own cooldown and fire bolt; summoned imps look like imps and die with the summoner
- A* routes around a wall
- lich immunity; the nova (wind-up, damage, the push by the lich's own STR, nothing outside the radius); Raise Dead (waits for its 8 s opening, then comes first; 3 per cast, 5 alive, disciples not counted); the gloom bolt volley; drain beams sent to a player who sees neither end
- the demon: each attack's animation starts with its wind-up (cleave `Melee`, swoop `Dash`, ring `Cast`) and runs through the recovery; the swoop keeps one `Dash` animation from the wind-up to the stop; the ring bursts at the end of its wind-up
- the hero's view (`vispoly.rs`): wall edges merge into one segment per straight wall, the polygon is a circle in an open room, a pillar casts a wedge, a closed door blocks, `inside` works across ±π and behind a pillar, the view sees floor behind a doorway beyond the tile FOV, walls by the FOV, a blind viewer its own tile; the shared `view` fixture (polygon points and visible tiles from 5 pixel positions)
- view-filtered snapshots; heroes' hits and explosions are sent without a position (shown out of view)
- shot hitboxes: a missile hits the ogre's chest but not over its head, the demon up to its horns, and passes over an orc's head
- 600-tick simulation of each boss without panics
- debug immortality and the boss path
- the sheet folding in the tree, items and keystones; `check_buy`, `respec` and the Progress v2 reader
- progress document: buying limits, saturating banking, `{}`, partial, unknown-field and broken documents, JSON round trip
- auth: token round trip, tampered, expired and wrong-secret tokens rejected; cookie parsing; Argon2id hash and verify; name and password rules; the limiter window
- client_ip: `TRUSTED_PROXIES` parsing (addresses, v4/v6 ranges, junk rejected); an untrusted peer's header is ignored; the rightmost entry wins; chained trusted proxies are skipped; a missing or garbage header keeps the peer; IPv4-mapped IPv6
- admin login: disabled without both variables or with a short password; name and password both checked; the `ta_admin` cookie is scoped to `/api/admin`, has no Max-Age and is separate from `ta_session` (a player cookie is no admin cookie); the idle timeout restarts with each action but not with background refreshes; `ADMIN_SESSION_MINUTES` parsing
- WebSocket origin check; only Vite's hashed bundles are cached for good
- telemetry: without an endpoint nothing is exported; a finished run is counted with level, boss, outcome and debug, and debug runs stay out of the gameplay counters (in-memory exporter)
- run record: outcome (won, lost, everyone left), every player, debug flag; leaving is not dying
- statistics (needs `DATABASE_URL`):
  - activity counts once per day and outlives the deleted account
  - today/7/30-day windows count backdated rows correctly and skip debug runs; levels with their bosses (also a run without a boss, and rows from before 0004), classes, tree-nodes histogram, top characters and the 30-day trend
  - online players get their account names
  - player search (`_` literal), sorting, paging and details
  - admin password change revokes sessions; any character can be deleted; the audit log is newest first
- database (needs `DATABASE_URL`):
  - names unique ignoring case
  - the 8-character cap and ownership
  - 20 bankings and 10 purchases in parallel lose nothing
  - old progress documents are read and saved as the current version
  - sessions end with logout and account deletion, which also removes the characters
  - sessions end when idle; activity restarts the clock; the end of a game connection extends a session but never revives a deleted one
- lobby:
  - only the host picks the boss, fixed after the start
  - rooms need a character, and characters switch only outside rooms
  - no shopping during a run, and the newest progress wins
  - a second login closes the first connection
  - `live()` counts online players, rooms and runs; who plays which character
  - a finished run banks on the played character only and is recorded (needs `DATABASE_URL`)
- XP and coin awards skip debug runs
- chests: touching opens once and pays the whole party
- mimics: hiding until touched, then hold before hunting; the boss waking leaves them asleep; they move only while airborne
- ts-rs export tests
- plan 3b: damage-type tiers, `replaces` and the cancels across tiers (`defs/effects.rs`, `run/effects.rs`); Entangled, Fear (a frightened monster runs and does not attack, also when its source is gone), Warded and Coated; the Staff of Rot's power 10; every shape plays as before (`every_shape_still_plays_today`); the 16 abilities in `run/abilities.rs` (Chain Spark's filters, Volley's three arrows, Smite, Whirl, the behind arc, the coat, Frost Nova and War Cry and Ground Slam, Consecrate, Entangle at walls, melee and with a strong target); debug weapons and the lift (`lobby.rs`); the fingerprints (`level_setup_is_unchanged`, `simulation_is_unchanged_after_waking_the_boss`, `plan_3a_fight_is_pinned`) did not change in plan 3b

**Client (`npm test`, 761 tests):**
- the run session (#302; `src/run/`): `session.test.ts` runs the real `RunSession` against fakes for the net, the HUD and the view only (`run/testing.ts`: `fakeNet`, `fakeHud` and `fakeView` record what is sent, called and drawn; `startInfo` builds a small run from ASCII rows, `snap` a snapshot). It covers messages and events (a `Tile` changes the map and then reaches the view, two sealed entrance tiles announce the force field once, `HeroChanged` reaches the predictor and the HUD, picture events go to the view and state events do not, `Coins`, the fallen message once, a charm that begins, debug keys outside a debug run, `destroy` unsubscribes), the fixed step (one `Input` per step with the host's movement, at most five steps per frame, a dead hero sends nothing but the latches are read, an open menu blocks abilities, the aim without a pointer, a predicted primary shot) and the frame (the camera target while alive and while spectating, the shots, the HUD frame with and without a view, the own data, `held` for A and D together, the weapon following the pointer between steps, the ended server shot ending the predicted copy, the charmed figure gliding from where it was drawn, dodge lines pruned before the view sees them). `shots.test.ts` covers `Shots` (a wall, running out of life, an ended own shot staying hidden, the server shot gone, another hero's shot not hidden) and `input.test.ts` `dir8` and `NO_INPUT`.
- the import guard (#302, #301; `src/render3d/imports.test.ts`): no file in `run/` or `render3d/` reaches `phaser` through non-type imports, followed transitively; `render3d/` has only `three/webgpu` and `three/tsl` as bare specifiers and `run/` none; the files the session and the 3D view import (`game/useHint.ts`, `game/terrain-layers.ts`, `game/world.ts`, `game/light.ts`, `game/woodland/ground.ts`, `ui/hud.ts`, `net.ts`) do not reach `phaser` either; `main.ts` has no static import of `render3d`. A failure names the chain (`run/session.ts > game/foo.ts > phaser`): cut the import, or move the part the session needs into a Phaser-free module as `game/effectBits.ts`, `game/terrain-layers.ts` and `game/darkvisionSteps.ts` were (the old file re-exports it). An import the test cannot resolve fails too, unless it is a known asset (`.css`, `.png`, `.json`, `.wgsl`, `.glsl`, `.svg`), so a new kind of import cannot slip past it silently.
- the 3D view (#301; `src/render3d/`): `camera.test.ts` (projection and its inverse at several pitches and yaws, agreement with a three.js `OrthographicCamera`, the screen-space snap, keys to world, facing); `quality.test.ts` (`effZoom` for zoom 2 to 8, `pickZoom`, `renderPlan` for odd and 1x1 canvases); `params.test.ts` (bad URL values give the default or the clamped value, the round trip, `wrapYaw` for in-range, wrapped and huge values); `urlWriter.test.ts` (many changes give one write with the last value); `demoMap.test.ts` and `worldMesh.test.ts` (the map's corridors, floor, top and face quads, winding, upright frames, uv inside its rect); `scene.test.ts` (`frameDt`'s clamp, the knight stopping and sliding at walls, walkers and their pauses, torches on wall faces); `cards.test.ts` (card quads at yaw 0 and 90°, the flipped u, glow counts, a camera-facing glow that projects to a square and lies nearer to the camera than its torch by its size); `light.test.ts` (flicker bounds, the light list, packing 8 slots); `panel.test.ts` (jsdom: the buttons, wrapping, pressed states, the debug button, Hide and Show); `stats.test.ts` (the F3 text); `recover.test.ts` (one rebuild at a time, the follow-up rebuild, the retry after 1 s, `onError` per failed attempt); and in `game/frameTimer.test.ts` the system names given by the caller and `renderEnd(n)`.
- figures, objects and the see-through (#303): `game/anim/pose.test.ts` (`figurePose`), `game/anim/figureState.test.ts` (`FigureStates`), `game/objectPose.test.ts` (chest, barrel, torch and mimic poses, `MimicPose.flip`); in `src/render3d/` `cards.test.ts` (screen-aligned shadows, lying and flat cards, wall cards, sort order), `figures.test.ts` (see the list below, plus the board objects and coins), `atlasBounds.test.ts`, `openers.test.ts` (`opensWalls` per kind and state; `seenOpeners` against a real sight), `seeThrough.test.ts` (`discStrength`, `wallKeep`, the Bayer values, `wallOpens`, `nearOf` along the ground, `discQuads` and the clamp), `materials.test.ts`, `galleryStage.test.ts` (the gallery's frame goes through `Figures3d` and `seenOpeners`), `panel.test.ts` and `params.test.ts` (the See through row and keys).
- the 3D world and run (#302; `src/render3d/`, `src/r3dFlag.ts`): `heights.test.ts` (a height for every `TILE_ID`, so a new tile fails until it has one; outside the map; the height bytes); `worldMesh.test.ts` (floor, faces on one, two and four sides, a lone pillar, wall between walls, `Wall` beside `Void` and at the map's edge, a corridor junction, a room corner and a corridor mouth against a brute-force count of exposed sides with every face on its side's plane, terrain gives no quad, doors are floor, woodland cells and `Barrier` give ground quads with image uv, a face whose neighbour is in the next chunk, narrow edge chunks and a map smaller than a chunk, `chunksOfTile` up to nine chunks, the frame shares over 20,000 cells, winding and uv inside the rect); `terrainMesh.test.ts` (shallow, deep, lava and chasm layers and their order, `terrainFrame`, `stepTerrainUv` writes only when a frame steps); `fieldMesh.test.ts` (sides only toward lower, unsealed cells); `light.test.ts` (`shoulder`, `stoneClear`, `cssColour`); `camera.test.ts` (`followClamp` at turn 0, 45° and 90°, at pitch 30°, on a map smaller than the view; `freeClamp`); `params.test.ts` and `panel.test.ts` (`free`, `light`, zoom 1, foreign URL keys kept, the run panel's buttons, `destroy`); `host.test.ts` (jsdom with a fake view: `stop` removes everything, two hosts leave one canvas, keys in an input are not the stage's, a rebuild reads the stage's world again, stop while `createView` is pending, `setParams` inside `makeStage`, a throwing `makeStage` or `built`, `stage.key` true prevents the default, a newer `__r3d` is left alone, error boxes are cleared); `groundJob.test.ts` (the painted image equals `paintGround` byte for byte, budget 0 still works, the spawn band first); `runView3d.test.ts` (`moveInput` at several yaws); `figures.test.ts` (every figure kind gives a body card and an armed one a weapon card, dead figures and the skull after a death, a lost one leaves none, a sleeper lies at `LIE_Y`, incorporeal figures in the translucent pass, hidden and own shots, `screenAim` and the facing dead zone, the melee step, wading crop, the dragon's `framePoint`, fade-in and fade-out of Invisible, dash afterimages, openers); `atlasBounds.test.ts`; `runStage.test.ts` (with the session's fakes: the stage's map is the session's, a tile change reaches the view, the ground is uploaded again after a rebuild and not repainted, the free camera stops the hero and moves the camera, the follow camera stays inside the map, the hero card runs by the session's animation also against a wall, buffered early messages are replayed, `stop` ends the pre-view interval); `r3dFlag.test.ts` (which page `?r3d` opens).
- plan 3b: `volleyShotIds`/`volleyAngles`/`cosmeticCount` (`game/world.test.ts`), the look following the sampled snapshot, `lookOf` and the Fear "!" (`game/status.test.ts`), `debugWeapons` and `debugReject` (`ui/lobby.test.ts`), the symbols (`ui/effectIcons.test.ts`), the wire decode of `looks` and `Zap`
- the wolf: trimmed wolf and knight frames (`scripts/figure-frames.test.ts`), the Recruit hint (`game/useHint.test.ts`), shots flying through non-Hostile creatures (`game/shotTargets.test.ts`), the HUD label (`ui/hud.test.ts`), the sleeper's Z on `DEPTH.sound` (`game/status.test.ts`), the cooldown prediction of `SummonWolf` (`game/world.test.ts`)
- spectres (#169, #170): `scripts/spectre-frames.test.ts` (8 frames of 16x16, on the bottom row at rest and running, 12 rows tall, the idle bob, the 16 atlas names, palette colours only, the Shade with the Ghost's shape), `game/shadeHaze.test.ts` (the veil's strands root inside the body and puff out round it standing, mirrored; some wrap in front; they stream behind a move and the cloud comes back; no overstretch; cleared without a target; the alpha steps; `strandPixels`, `veilPixels` core and cloth, `silhouetteAlpha` over `AMBUSH_WARNING`, `dissolveVeil` spreading and fading, `restingVeil`), `game/anim/entityAlpha.test.ts` (Ghost pulse, flicker, phase)
- `game/damage.test.ts`: the number colour per damage type (physical white on enemies, red on heroes).
- `pickFrame.test.ts`: the dragon's and the demon's frame choice (wind-up frame with the telegraph, strike frames after it, the demon's charge loop and ring burst).
- The collision and FOV ports must equal the Rust fixtures exactly. The fixture map holds every kind of terrain, and 10 random walks with dashes and pushes (14–44 px, any direction) cross it.
- `wire.test.ts` (it also covers the `act` input field and `Dmg` with `ty` and `imm`) unpacks the sample snapshots from `wire-fixtures.json` (every event type, a spectator, with and without boss bar) and compares them with their readable form: positions within 1/32 px, aim within half a step, everything else exact. It also checks the aim range and `packInput`.
- `game/light.test.ts`: the static light map equals the server's on the shared fixture (`fixtures.json`, every lit tile identical); `addMoving` marks exactly the server's tiles for five moving lights on the fixture map (open floor, beside a corner, against a wall, on a tile corner, a magic light); switching, recolouring, removing and map changes; colours add per channel and cap at 255; a torch and lava mix to orange, with more red than the torch alone; the falloff (1 at the centre, 0.1 at the edge, half way down at half the radius, never brighter further out).
- `sim/vispoly.test.ts`: `wallSegments` merges each straight wall into one segment; the visibility polygon is a circle in an open room (also from a tile corner) and a half-tile circle for a blind hero (`sightRadius(0)` = 8 px); a pillar casts a wedge whose rays stop on its near face while the rays beside it reach the radius; it stays inside a corridor; a closed door blocks, an open one does not; a room without walls stops at the map edge; with the angular buckets the polygon is exactly that of casting every ray against every segment (20 maps of scattered pillars, viewers inside tiles and on tile edges and corners, #280).
- `sim/view.test.ts`: `inside` by distance and across ±π (the closing edge) and behind a pillar; the `View` sees floor behind a doorway beyond the tile FOV (the final review's case), shows walls by the FOV and nothing off the map, a blind viewer sees its own tile, `version` counts computes; on the shared fixture (5 viewers on the fixture map) the visible tiles equal the server's and the polygon points match to 1e-6.
- `game/lightview.test.ts` (also: `seenForLight`: the view, plus open tiles next to an open tile in view, never walls, tiles two steps away or tiles next to a wall in view; the darkvision tile rule `tileDark` (a tile in reach is never darker than `DV_DARK`, so a light rim gets no dark ring), the greyscale strength `dvMask` (1 where only darkvision shows a tile, fading to 0 as light takes over), `darkReach`, `nextDarkvision` 0 -> 0.5 -> 1 -> 0): black where not seen or not lit, a lit tile is never fully black (`MIN_LIT` 0.08) and a dim one stays dim, flicker stays around 1 by kind; the Catmull-Rom weights sum to 1 and pass through the tile centres. `game/lightshader.test.ts` (texel packing: rounded and clamped; the GLSL weights are `catmullRom`'s; the `DV` variant). `game/texupload.test.ts` (`copyWindow` packs the rows of a rectangle).
- `net.test.ts`: the `?lag` simulator delivers 2,000 messages in order under a timer that rounds delays down like a browser (the old one-timer-per-message line failed it).
- `ui/endText.test.ts`: the end screen's first line (the slain boss on a dungeon win, the level's defeat line, the level's victory line when there is no boss).
- `game/world.test.ts`: the predictor replays the moves it sent while it predicted a fall, so a debug climb-out the server already did is followed exactly.
- `game/status.test.ts`: the lying pose and layout (rotation, crop, which side), the bitmask helper; `ui/effectIcons.test.ts`: every effect has a symbol and the symbols are distinct; `game/world.test.ts` also checks the predictor's no-act and no-turn flags

**Browser smoke test (`tools/e2e/smoke.mjs`):**
- two headless browser contexts register throwaway accounts (`e2e_<hex>`) and create a character each of the given classes, then create and join a run, start, move and attack
- a third context logs into the first account, which must close the first window ("another window"). It runs in a second browser process: in the shared one its WebGL start queues behind the two running SwiftShader games and blocks its page for about 30 s (#175)
- both accounts are deleted at the end (the register, login, debug-mark, character and delete helpers are in `tools/e2e/account.mjs`, shared with `r3d-run.mjs`)
- with `ADMIN_USER` and `ADMIN_PASSWORD` set (the same as the server's), an admin pass follows:
  1. log in at `/admin` and find the online account on the dashboard;
  2. delete its character, which logs the player out;
  3. change its password, after which the old one fails and the new one works;
  4. delete the player through the confirmation form;
  5. check the audit log.

  It takes screenshots of the dashboard (light and dark), the player page and the audit log.
- only when the query contains `debug` (the script's own switch; both throwaway accounts are marked as debug accounts first, through the admin API when `ADMIN_USER`/`ADMIN_PASSWORD` are set, else with psql): presses F4 twice and waits for the F3 panel to show `darkvision 50%`, then `100%` (screenshot `5-darkvision.png`); a failure there is logged, not thrown, so the accounts are still deleted
- with a sixth argument `woodland`, the host clicks the Woodland level card (the boss panel must disappear and the guest must see the choice) before starting, and a screenshot of the level panel is taken; the default is the dungeon
- screenshots (login, characters, lobby, room, game) go to the output folder; prints the F3 stats and any browser errors (the expected 401 of the first `/api/me` is ignored)

**Client admin tests (`src/admin/charts.test.ts`):** nice axis maximum and ticks, the rounded-top column path, number, duration and share formatting.

## 13. Known limitations

- The 3D view (#302): the HUD's F3 panel in a `?r3d` run shows the draw calls and the canvas size but not the backend and the render size (the dev readout has them); the HUD shows the debug message and the F4–F9 key list in a 3D run although the 3D host passes no debug keys yet (#311). three's clear does not reach the canvas where nothing is drawn (WebGL2, real graphics card); the canvas's CSS background hides it (§9). A failed load of the 3D code returns to the lobby without sending `LeaveRun`, so the server counts the debug player in that run until it ends. Terrain layers lie 0.002 tiles apart and may shimmer at a low pitch (fallback: draw order).
- Animals (#227): a Neutral-side creature's projectiles hit nothing, and a hazard ability used by a Neutral creature reaches `unreachable!` (`run/abilities.rs`). No animal shoots or casts today; both must be fixed before one does. Numbers of the animals (`WILD_MIN_FLEE`, `WILD_CALM_DIST`, HP, speeds) are first guesses.
- Woodland (#203): all its numbers are first guesses for the balancing pass (`WOODLAND_END_XP` 150, group sizes, the road and clearing shapes, tree density, the animals). The forest runs on 24 tiles past the invisible walls at both ends (#259); the camera stops at the map's edge, so on a screen more than about 54 tiles wide (at its zoom) the hero is a little off-centre next to a wall. In daylight beyond sight is hazy (`DEFAULT_HAZE`, #278) and creatures beyond it stay hidden (#258). The haze behind a trunk covers ground where the server sends no creature: that is by design (the creature behind the trunk is hidden by the server, the ground is only hazed). The sun range (`SUN_ELEVATION`), the shadow constants (`SHADOW_X`, `SHADOW_Y`, `SHADOW_ALPHA`, the contact ellipse) and every `DEFAULT_HAZE` number were approved by the user in `?woodland` on the phone (2026-11-08; the picked values are listed in the Design comment on #277); the balancing pass may still revisit them. The plants are drawn a few per frame, so the first seconds of a run show bare ground (and no shade) near the edges of the window until they are drawn; headless software rendering is slower still.
- Pack art gaps: no attack frames, no up/down facing. (The dragon, the demon and the lich have their own frames.)
- No sound.
- Damage types, resistances and the barrel numbers are first guesses for the balancing pass, and the user has not played the barrels yet (see the `balancing` issues).
- Lighting: all numbers (radius, intensity, flicker per kind, `TORCH_SPACING`, `DARK_SHARE`, `MIN_LIT`, `WARM`, the falloff edge, `BLAST_FLASH`, `HOLY_FLASH`) are first guesses for the balancing pass. The user approved the overall look (2026-10-15).
- The light picture is smoothed up from per-tile values (on the GPU) and dark tiles are set back to black, so the light-to-dark step sits on the tile boundary: the LINEAR filter on the picture softens it by half a picture pixel (a quarter tile) on each side, and the lit tile next to a dark one darkens towards it.
- **Vision edges:** the client computes the view from its predicted (or interpolated) position, the server from its own, so a tile at the very edge can differ for a moment. A monster on a seen tile can stand partly in the drawn black (a tile counts when any of its 16 sample points is in sight). Walls count per tile by the tile FOV, so a wall the FOV marks can show whole although the polygon's shadow covers its face, and a wall face the polygon reaches outside the FOV stays black.
- The moving-light list uses a distance test without line of sight, so the glow of a shot or enemy behind a wall within range is sent and drawn (its carrier is not).
- An explosion's (and a healing wave's) flash is flat for its 0.4 s (0.6 s) and then gone (no fade-out). A shot without an explosion lights nothing at its impact once its own light is gone.
- Vision and darkvision: every number (vision per enemy in tiles, the boss 25 tiles, darkvision shares 0 / 1/3 / 1, the greyscale `DV_DARK` 0.45) is a first guess for the balancing pass. The humans' blindness in the dark makes the lich fight easier (disciples idle in the dark).
- The greyscale (`DarkvisionFX`) is a camera-wide post effect, so inside the darkvision range it also greys world-space damage numbers, HP bars and the E hint, not only the floor and figures. Per-object exclusion would need a second camera or layer. The haze (`UnseenFX`, #278) is a camera post effect too: where it falls on unseen space it also hazes numbers, HP bars and the E hint there, and teammates beyond sight (they are always sent) are hazed with the ground, which is consistent.
- Light is refreshed once per tick before the AI (perception needs this tick's lights) and once more in `send_snapshots` on snapshot ticks. Positions move between the two; the snapshot refresh is the one the client must match, so the filter uses it. It costs one extra moving-light cast per snapshot.
- Each attach of `DarkvisionFX` compiles a new pipeline instance (F4 and the `?lights` V key cycle it, so this only happens in debug use); the mask texture is not primed at `LightView` construction.
- Hero melee, dashes and arrows next to a torch put it out (any non-heat hit does); this is the agreed rule, but it may prove annoying in play.
- Incorporeal: the resistances (magical −10 %), the 0.6 alpha, the 0.35 alpha of an invisible party hero and the vanish and fade times are first guesses. The Ghost (#169) and the Shade (#170) are the incorporeal creatures; F7 makes any other enemy one. Parties without magic can barely hurt them (the Barbarian has no magical damage), by decision, until heroes get magic weapons.
- Effects: the durations, the lich's cooldowns and the bosses' 0.5 effect resistances are first guesses for the balancing pass, and the lich's spells and the HUD icons have not been played live yet. The HUD icons are drawn symbols (`ui/effectIcons.ts`). Petrified uses a greyscale filter, so it shows only with WebGL. No effect has a sound, and Slowed has no source yet (only F8 applies it); Accelerated comes from Haste and War Cry, Frozen from Frost Nova, Poisoned from the Staff of Rot and Poison Coat (plan 3b). The status marks follow the figure's alpha and vanish with the effects when a creature starts to fall or drown.
- Torches are sent only when in the viewer's FOV, so a torch sprite can pop in at the edge of the FOV (under the darkness, so it is rarely noticeable). The lights themselves come from the start info and events and never pop.
- Weapon effects (swooshes, rings, particles) are drawn under the darkness, so they are black where it is dark, like the heroes (an explosion lights its own spot). The server still sends hurt and heal on a hero in the dark (`on_hero`); the client draws numbers on heroes and allies over the darkness, so they show, and numbers on monsters under it, so they vanish where it is dark.
- Snapshots are compact but not delta-compressed (every snapshot is complete). They are small anyway.
- Boss fights are covered by unit tests and were played live once against all three bosses; their numbers are not balanced yet.
- No password recovery, by design: there is no e-mail address to send anything to.
- Passwords travel in the login request. In production the game must run behind a TLS reverse proxy (with `COOKIE_SECURE=1`).
- Behind a reverse proxy the per-IP limits need `TRUSTED_PROXIES` (§11a); without it every request comes from the proxy's IP and the limits apply to everyone together.
- The login limiter and the one-connection-per-account rule live in the memory of one server process.
- Admin sessions also live in memory: a restart logs the admin out. They end after `ADMIN_SESSION_MINUTES` without an admin action. There is one admin login, shared by whoever knows it; the audit log names it, not a person.
- Statistics count UTC days. Their history starts with the first deployment of the admin area.
- OpenTelemetry export is plain HTTP only (no TLS client built in).
